### 词群 06.01｜从技术人才到发明成果 {#group-06-01}

**串联方式：** 先认技术、技法与工程人才，再经历进步、创新、发明、发现及专利申请。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">technology<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-001" aria-label="定位 technology">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-001" data-mark-label="technology" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="technology：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="technology：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=technology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 technology" title="在有道查询 technology">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 技术</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An engineer at the polytechnic taught a mechanic a technique using laser <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=technology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>technology</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">理工学院的一位工程师教了一名机械师一种使用激光技术的方法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The polytechnic invited an engineer and a mechanic to demonstrate a new technique in automotive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=technology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>technology</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">理工学院邀请一位工程师和一名机械师演示汽车技术中的一种新方法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mechanic and an engineer refined the technique before introducing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=technology&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>technology</strong></a> at a polytechnic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一名机械师与一位工程师完善了该方法，然后在一所理工学院推广这项技术。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">renewable energy technology</span> · 可再生能源技术</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engineering&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">engineering</a></span> · 工程学</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workshop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">workshop</a></span> · 车间</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-006">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-006" class="vocabulary-word-title">advance<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-006" aria-label="定位 advance">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-006" data-mark-label="advance" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="advance：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="advance：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 advance" title="在有道查询 advance">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-006</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.发展；前进；v. 发展；前进</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advance</strong></a> battery research, companies must innovate rather than wait for a breakthrough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为推动电池研究，公司必须创新，而非等待突破。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A breakthrough can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advance</strong></a> an entire industry and encourage smaller firms to innovate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次突破能够推动整个行业发展，并鼓励小公司创新。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists who innovate gradually may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advance</strong></a> knowledge without achieving a dramatic breakthrough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逐步创新的科学家即使未取得重大突破，也可能推动知识进步。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an advance in medicine</span> · 医学进展</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=progress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">progress</a></span> · 进步</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-007">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-007" class="vocabulary-word-title">innovate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-007" aria-label="定位 innovate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-007" data-mark-label="innovate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="innovate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="innovate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 innovate" title="在有道查询 innovate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-007</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 创新，改革</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To advance battery research, companies must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>innovate</strong></a> rather than wait for a breakthrough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为推动电池研究，公司必须创新，而非等待突破。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A breakthrough can advance an entire industry and encourage smaller firms to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>innovate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次突破能够推动整个行业发展，并鼓励小公司创新。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>innovate</strong></a> gradually may advance knowledge without achieving a dramatic breakthrough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逐步创新的科学家即使未取得重大突破，也可能推动知识进步。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">innovate continuously</span> · 不断创新</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=progress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">progress</a></span> · 进步</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">breakthrough<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-008" aria-label="定位 breakthrough">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-008" data-mark-label="breakthrough" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="breakthrough：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="breakthrough：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breakthrough&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 breakthrough" title="在有道查询 breakthrough">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 突破</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To advance battery research, companies must innovate rather than wait for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breakthrough&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breakthrough</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为推动电池研究，公司必须创新，而非等待突破。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breakthrough&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breakthrough</strong></a> can advance an entire industry and encourage smaller firms to innovate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次突破能够推动整个行业发展，并鼓励小公司创新。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scientists who innovate gradually may advance knowledge without achieving a dramatic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=breakthrough&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>breakthrough</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逐步创新的科学家即使未取得重大突破，也可能推动知识进步。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a technological breakthrough</span> · 技术突破</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=progress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">progress</a></span> · 进步</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-009">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-009" class="vocabulary-word-title">gizmo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-009" aria-label="定位 gizmo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-009" data-mark-label="gizmo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gizmo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gizmo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gizmo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gizmo" title="在有道查询 gizmo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-009</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 小装置</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To invent a useful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gizmo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gizmo</strong></a>, we must discover better materials and devise a design worthy of a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要发明实用的小装置，我们必须发现更好的材料，并设计出值得申请专利的方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An engineer may discover a fault, devise a remedy, and invent a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gizmo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gizmo</strong></a> protected by a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师可能发现故障、设计补救方法，并发明出受专利保护的小装置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They hope to discover a new coating, devise a cheaper process and invent a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gizmo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gizmo</strong></a> eligible for a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们希望发现新涂层、设计更廉价的工艺，并发明可申请专利的小装置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a useful gizmo</span> · 实用小装置</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">invention</a></span> · 发明</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prototype&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prototype</a></span> · 原型</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">patent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-010" aria-label="定位 patent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-010" data-mark-label="patent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="patent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="patent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 patent" title="在有道查询 patent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 专利，专利权；v. 得到……的专利权；给……专利证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To invent a useful gizmo, we must discover better materials and devise a design worthy of a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patent</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要发明实用的小装置，我们必须发现更好的材料，并设计出值得申请专利的方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An engineer may discover a fault, devise a remedy, and invent a gizmo protected by a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patent</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师可能发现故障、设计补救方法，并发明出受专利保护的小装置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They hope to discover a new coating, devise a cheaper process and invent a gizmo eligible for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patent</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们希望发现新涂层、设计更廉价的工艺，并发明可申请专利的小装置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">apply for a patent</span> · 申请专利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">invention</a></span> · 发明</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prototype&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prototype</a></span> · 原型</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-011">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 设计，发明</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To invent a useful gizmo, we must discover better materials and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devise</strong></a> a design worthy of a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要发明实用的小装置，我们必须发现更好的材料，并设计出值得申请专利的方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An engineer may discover a fault, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devise</strong></a> a remedy, and invent a gizmo protected by a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师可能发现故障、设计补救方法，并发明出受专利保护的小装置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They hope to discover a new coating, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=devise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>devise</strong></a> a cheaper process and invent a gizmo eligible for a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们希望发现新涂层、设计更廉价的工艺，并发明可申请专利的小装置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">devise a solution</span> · 设计解决方案</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">invention</a></span> · 发明</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prototype&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prototype</a></span> · 原型</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An engineer may discover a fault, devise a remedy, and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invent</strong></a> a gizmo protected by a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师可能发现故障、设计补救方法，并发明出受专利保护的小装置。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To invent a useful gizmo, we must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discover</strong></a> better materials and devise a design worthy of a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要发明实用的小装置，我们必须发现更好的材料，并设计出值得申请专利的方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An engineer may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discover</strong></a> a fault, devise a remedy, and invent a gizmo protected by a patent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师可能发现故障、设计补救方法，并发明出受专利保护的小装置。</p></li>

### 词群 06.02｜怎样公开并专注研究领域 {#group-06-02}

**串联方式：** 区分揭示、公开和暴露，再确定领域与基础，最后专注某个方向。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">disclose<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-014" aria-label="定位 disclose">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-014" data-mark-label="disclose" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="disclose：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="disclose：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 disclose" title="在有道查询 disclose">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 揭露，透露</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An independent audit may uncover defects, reveal their cause, expose safety risks and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disclose</strong></a> hidden costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立审计可能发现缺陷、揭示原因、揭露安全风险并披露隐性成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will uncover the facts, reveal its findings, expose any fraud and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disclose</strong></a> the evidence publicly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将查明事实、公布发现、揭露任何欺诈并公开证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tests can uncover a weakness and reveal how to fix it before engineers expose the device to stress or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disclose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disclose</strong></a> results.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试能发现薄弱点并揭示修复办法，然后工程师才会让设备承受压力或公布结果。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">disclose information</span> · 披露信息</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disclosure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disclosure</a></span> · 披露</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">reveal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-015" aria-label="定位 reveal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-015" data-mark-label="reveal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reveal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reveal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reveal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reveal" title="在有道查询 reveal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 展现；显示，透露</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An independent audit may uncover defects, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reveal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reveal</strong></a> their cause, expose safety risks and disclose hidden costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立审计可能发现缺陷、揭示原因、揭露安全风险并披露隐性成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will uncover the facts, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reveal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reveal</strong></a> its findings, expose any fraud and disclose the evidence publicly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将查明事实、公布发现、揭露任何欺诈并公开证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tests can uncover a weakness and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reveal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reveal</strong></a> how to fix it before engineers expose the device to stress or disclose results.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试能发现薄弱点并揭示修复办法，然后工程师才会让设备承受压力或公布结果。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reveal a flaw</span> · 揭示缺陷</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disclosure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">disclosure</a></span> · 披露</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An independent audit may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=uncover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>uncover</strong></a> defects, reveal their cause, expose safety risks and disclose hidden costs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立审计可能发现缺陷、揭示原因、揭露安全风险并披露隐性成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=uncover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>uncover</strong></a> the facts, reveal its findings, expose any fraud and disclose the evidence publicly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将查明事实、公布发现、揭露任何欺诈并公开证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tests can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=uncover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>uncover</strong></a> a weakness and reveal how to fix it before engineers expose the device to stress or disclose results.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试能发现薄弱点并揭示修复办法，然后工程师才会让设备承受压力或公布结果。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will uncover the facts, reveal its findings, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expose</strong></a> any fraud and disclose the evidence publicly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将查明事实、公布发现、揭露任何欺诈并公开证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Tests can uncover a weakness and reveal how to fix it before engineers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expose</strong></a> the device to stress or disclose results.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试能发现薄弱点并揭示修复办法，然后工程师才会让设备承受压力或公布结果。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-018">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-018" class="vocabulary-word-title">domain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-018" aria-label="定位 domain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-018" data-mark-label="domain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="domain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="domain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=domain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 domain" title="在有道查询 domain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-018</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(活动、学问等的)领域；领土，领地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A solid foundation in physics helps researchers enter a new field within the broader realm of technology and its scientific <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=domain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>domain</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扎实的物理基础有助于研究者进入技术及其科学范畴中的新研究领域。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In this field, basic mathematics provides the foundation for work in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=domain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>domain</strong></a> of robotics and the realm of artificial intelligence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个领域，基础数学为机器人领域和人工智能领域的工作奠定基础。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her foundation in biology supports a research field that crosses the medical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=domain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>domain</strong></a> and the realm of engineering.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的生物学基础支撑着一项跨越医学领域和工程领域的研究。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the public domain</span> · 公共领域</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discipline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">discipline</a></span> · 学科</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">specialism</a></span> · 专长</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-019">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 领域</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A solid foundation in physics helps researchers enter a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=field&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>field</strong></a> within the broader realm of technology and its scientific domain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扎实的物理基础有助于研究者进入技术及其科学范畴中的新研究领域。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=field&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>field</strong></a>, basic mathematics provides the foundation for work in the domain of robotics and the realm of artificial intelligence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个领域，基础数学为机器人领域和人工智能领域的工作奠定基础。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her foundation in biology supports a research <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=field&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>field</strong></a> that crosses the medical domain and the realm of engineering.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的生物学基础支撑着一项跨越医学领域和工程领域的研究。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a research field</span> · 研究领域</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discipline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">discipline</a></span> · 学科</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">specialism</a></span> · 专长</li></ul>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In this field, basic mathematics provides the foundation for work in the domain of robotics and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=realm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>realm</strong></a> of artificial intelligence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个领域，基础数学为机器人领域和人工智能领域的工作奠定基础。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her foundation in biology supports a research field that crosses the medical domain and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=realm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>realm</strong></a> of engineering.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的生物学基础支撑着一项跨越医学领域和工程领域的研究。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 基础；地基；基金会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A solid <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=foundation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>foundation</strong></a> in physics helps researchers enter a new field within the broader realm of technology and its scientific domain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扎实的物理基础有助于研究者进入技术及其科学范畴中的新研究领域。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In this field, basic mathematics provides the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=foundation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>foundation</strong></a> for work in the domain of robotics and the realm of artificial intelligence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个领域，基础数学为机器人领域和人工智能领域的工作奠定基础。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=foundation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>foundation</strong></a> in biology supports a research field that crosses the medical domain and the realm of engineering.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的生物学基础支撑着一项跨越医学领域和工程领域的研究。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a solid foundation</span> · 牢固基础</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discipline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">discipline</a></span> · 学科</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">specialism</a></span> · 专长</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">specialise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-022" aria-label="定位 specialise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-022" data-mark-label="specialise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="specialise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="specialise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 specialise" title="在有道查询 specialise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(= specialize)；v. 专攻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>specialise</strong></a> in optics must absorb complex ideas, concentrate during experiments and focus on precision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专攻光学的学生必须吸收复杂概念，实验时集中注意力，并注重精度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>specialise</strong></a> successfully, learn to absorb feedback, concentrate on difficult tasks and focus on a clear research question.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要成功专精一个领域，须学会吸收反馈、专心处理难题，并聚焦明确的研究问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>specialise</strong></a> over time as they absorb experience, concentrate their efforts and focus on a particular application.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师通过吸收经验、集中精力和聚焦特定应用，逐渐形成专长。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">specialise in robotics</span> · 专攻机器人学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concentration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">concentration</a></span> · 专注</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attention</a></span> · 注意力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 06.03｜用工具测试与评估 {#group-06-03}

**串联方式：** 从利用和应用进入设备、设施和仪器，再进行测量、计算和评价。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-026">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-026" class="vocabulary-word-title">utilise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-026" aria-label="定位 utilise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-026" data-mark-label="utilise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="utilise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="utilise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=utilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 utilise" title="在有道查询 utilise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-026</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(= utilize)；v. 利用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=utilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>utilise</strong></a> a tester to monitor energy usage and apply the results to product design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们利用测试仪监测能源使用量，并将结果应用于产品设计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Technicians apply this method and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=utilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>utilise</strong></a> a portable tester to measure electricity usage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术人员应用这种方法，利用便携式测试仪测量用电量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To apply the new standard, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=utilise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>utilise</strong></a> a calibrated tester and record daily power usage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要应用新标准，需使用校准过的测试仪，并记录每日用电量。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">utilise resources</span> · 利用资源</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">efficiency</a></span> · 效率</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We utilise a tester to monitor energy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=usage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>usage</strong></a> and apply the results to product design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们利用测试仪监测能源使用量，并将结果应用于产品设计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Technicians apply this method and utilise a portable tester to measure electricity <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=usage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>usage</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术人员应用这种方法，利用便携式测试仪测量用电量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To apply the new standard, utilise a calibrated tester and record daily power <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=usage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>usage</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要应用新标准，需使用校准过的测试仪，并记录每日用电量。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We utilise a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tester&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tester</strong></a> to monitor energy usage and apply the results to product design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们利用测试仪监测能源使用量，并将结果应用于产品设计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Technicians apply this method and utilise a portable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tester&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tester</strong></a> to measure electricity usage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术人员应用这种方法，利用便携式测试仪测量用电量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To apply the new standard, utilise a calibrated <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tester&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tester</strong></a> and record daily power usage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要应用新标准，需使用校准过的测试仪，并记录每日用电量。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a voltage tester</span> · 电压测试仪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">efficiency</a></span> · 效率</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We utilise a tester to monitor energy usage and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apply&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>apply</strong></a> the results to product design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们利用测试仪监测能源使用量，并将结果应用于产品设计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Technicians <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apply&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>apply</strong></a> this method and utilise a portable tester to measure electricity usage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术人员应用这种方法，利用便携式测试仪测量用电量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apply&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>apply</strong></a> the new standard, utilise a calibrated tester and record daily power usage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要应用新标准，需使用校准过的测试仪，并记录每日用电量。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-030">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 应用；(家用)器具</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The testing facility checks each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appliance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appliance</strong></a> with specialist equipment, a measuring instrument and an insulated tool.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试设施使用专业设备、测量仪器和绝缘工具检查每件电器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the facility, trainees distinguish a household <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appliance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appliance</strong></a> from laboratory equipment, a scientific instrument and a hand tool.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在设施内，学员区分家用电器、实验室设备、科学仪器和手工工具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bring the faulty <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appliance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appliance</strong></a> to our facility, where suitable equipment, an instrument and a repair tool are available.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请把故障电器带到我们的设施，那里备有合适的设备、仪器和维修工具。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=facility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>facility</strong></a>, trainees distinguish a household appliance from laboratory equipment, a scientific instrument and a hand tool.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在设施内，学员区分家用电器、实验室设备、科学仪器和手工工具。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-035">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-035" class="vocabulary-word-title">gauge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-035" aria-label="定位 gauge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-035" data-mark-label="gauge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gauge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gauge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gauge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gauge" title="在有道查询 gauge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-035</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 测量仪器；v. 测量；判断</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gauge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gauge</strong></a> to measure pressure, count the readings, compute their average and calculate the error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用压力表测量压力，清点读数，计算平均值及误差。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We count each component, measure it with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gauge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gauge</strong></a>, compute its volume and calculate the material cost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们清点每个零件，用量规测量，算出体积并计算材料成本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sensor can measure strain, count cycles, compute fatigue and calculate when the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gauge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gauge</strong></a> needs replacement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">传感器能测量应变、计数循环、计算疲劳程度，并算出应变量规何时需更换。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 06.04｜部件怎样组装 {#group-06-04}

**串联方式：** 区分配件、副产品与辅助部件，再做添加、积累、装配和连接；belong 说明归属。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-042">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-042" class="vocabulary-word-title">accessory<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-042" aria-label="定位 accessory">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-042" data-mark-label="accessory" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="accessory：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="accessory：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accessory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 accessory" title="在有道查询 accessory">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-042</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 附件，配件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This versatile machine turns an industrial byproduct into an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accessory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accessory</strong></a> powered by an auxiliary motor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台多用途机器将工业副产品制成由辅助电机驱动的配件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An auxiliary heater allows the versatile dryer to process a wet byproduct used in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accessory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accessory</strong></a> manufacture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辅助加热器使这台多用途干燥机能够加工用于制造配件的湿副产品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The versatile printer uses a byproduct of paper production to make an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accessory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accessory</strong></a> for an auxiliary pump.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台多用途打印机使用造纸副产品制作辅助泵的配件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an optional accessory</span> · 可选配件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attachment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attachment</a></span> · 附件</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptable</a></span> · 适应性强的</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This versatile machine turns an industrial byproduct into an accessory powered by an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auxiliary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>auxiliary</strong></a> motor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台多用途机器将工业副产品制成由辅助电机驱动的配件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auxiliary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>auxiliary</strong></a> heater allows the versatile dryer to process a wet byproduct used in accessory manufacture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辅助加热器使这台多用途干燥机能够加工用于制造配件的湿副产品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The versatile printer uses a byproduct of paper production to make an accessory for an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=auxiliary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>auxiliary</strong></a> pump.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台多用途打印机使用造纸副产品制作辅助泵的配件。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An auxiliary heater allows the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=versatile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>versatile</strong></a> dryer to process a wet byproduct used in accessory manufacture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辅助加热器使这台多用途干燥机能够加工用于制造配件的湿副产品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=versatile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>versatile</strong></a> printer uses a byproduct of paper production to make an accessory for an auxiliary pump.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台多用途打印机使用造纸副产品制作辅助泵的配件。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-046">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-046" class="vocabulary-word-title">add<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-046" aria-label="定位 add">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-046" data-mark-label="add" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="add：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="add：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=add&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 add" title="在有道查询 add">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-046</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 添加；附加</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gather the parts, assemble the sensor, attach the cable and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=add&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>add</strong></a> power before you accumulate data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集零件、组装传感器、连接电缆并接通电源，然后再积累数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We gather materials and accumulate spare parts, then assemble the frame, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=add&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>add</strong></a> wheels and attach a handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们收集材料并积存备用零件，然后组装框架、加上轮子并装上把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To accumulate reliable readings, gather suitable sensors, assemble the circuit, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=add&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>add</strong></a> a battery and attach the probes securely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为积累可靠读数，需收集合适的传感器、组装电路、加装电池并牢固连接探针。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">add a component</span> · 添加零件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assembly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assembly</a></span> · 组装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connection</a></span> · 连接</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-047">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-047" class="vocabulary-word-title">accumulate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-047" aria-label="定位 accumulate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-047" data-mark-label="accumulate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="accumulate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="accumulate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accumulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 accumulate" title="在有道查询 accumulate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-047</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 积累；(数额)逐渐增加</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gather the parts, assemble the sensor, attach the cable and add power before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accumulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accumulate</strong></a> data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集零件、组装传感器、连接电缆并接通电源，然后再积累数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We gather materials and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accumulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accumulate</strong></a> spare parts, then assemble the frame, add wheels and attach a handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们收集材料并积存备用零件，然后组装框架、加上轮子并装上把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accumulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accumulate</strong></a> reliable readings, gather suitable sensors, assemble the circuit, add a battery and attach the probes securely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为积累可靠读数，需收集合适的传感器、组装电路、加装电池并牢固连接探针。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">accumulate data</span> · 积累数据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assembly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assembly</a></span> · 组装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connection</a></span> · 连接</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-048">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.组装；装配</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gather the parts, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assemble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assemble</strong></a> the sensor, attach the cable and add power before you accumulate data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集零件、组装传感器、连接电缆并接通电源，然后再积累数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We gather materials and accumulate spare parts, then <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assemble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assemble</strong></a> the frame, add wheels and attach a handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们收集材料并积存备用零件，然后组装框架、加上轮子并装上把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To accumulate reliable readings, gather suitable sensors, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assemble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assemble</strong></a> the circuit, add a battery and attach the probes securely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为积累可靠读数，需收集合适的传感器、组装电路、加装电池并牢固连接探针。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">assemble a model</span> · 组装模型</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assembly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assembly</a></span> · 组装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connection</a></span> · 连接</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-049">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Gather</strong></a> the parts, assemble the sensor, attach the cable and add power before you accumulate data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集零件、组装传感器、连接电缆并接通电源，然后再积累数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gather</strong></a> materials and accumulate spare parts, then assemble the frame, add wheels and attach a handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们收集材料并积存备用零件，然后组装框架、加上轮子并装上把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To accumulate reliable readings, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gather</strong></a> suitable sensors, assemble the circuit, add a battery and attach the probes securely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为积累可靠读数，需收集合适的传感器、组装电路、加装电池并牢固连接探针。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">gather materials</span> · 收集材料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assembly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assembly</a></span> · 组装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connection</a></span> · 连接</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">attach<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-050" aria-label="定位 attach">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-050" data-mark-label="attach" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="attach：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="attach：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 attach" title="在有道查询 attach">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 使依附；附加；缚，系，贴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gather the parts, assemble the sensor, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attach</strong></a> the cable and add power before you accumulate data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收集零件、组装传感器、连接电缆并接通电源，然后再积累数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We gather materials and accumulate spare parts, then assemble the frame, add wheels and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attach</strong></a> a handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们收集材料并积存备用零件，然后组装框架、加上轮子并装上把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To accumulate reliable readings, gather suitable sensors, assemble the circuit, add a battery and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attach</strong></a> the probes securely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为积累可靠读数，需收集合适的传感器、组装电路、加装电池并牢固连接探针。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">attach a cable</span> · 连接电缆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assembly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assembly</a></span> · 组装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connection</a></span> · 连接</li></ul>
</div>
