<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-051">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">belong<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-051" aria-label="定位 belong">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-051" data-mark-label="belong" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="belong：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="belong：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=belong&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 belong" title="在有道查询 belong">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 应在(某处)；适合(某种情形)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">These tools <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=belong&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>belong</strong></a> to the maintenance department.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些工具属于维修部门。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Where does this spare component <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=belong&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>belong</strong></a>?</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个备用零件该放在哪里？</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The discovery does not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=belong&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>belong</strong></a> to any single researcher.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这一发现不属于任何一位研究者个人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">belong to a team</span> · 属于一个团队</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ownership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ownership</a></span> · 所有权</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=member&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">member</a></span> · 成员</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 06.05｜光学、探测与感知 {#group-06-05}

**串联方式：** 从显微镜和镜片到雷达、回声与传感器，比较不同感知工具。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-052">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-052" class="vocabulary-word-title">optics<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-052" aria-label="定位 optics">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-052" data-mark-label="optics" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="optics：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="optics：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=optics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 optics" title="在有道查询 optics">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-052</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 光学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=optics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>optics</strong></a> class, we adjusted the lens of a microscope to sharpen the image.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学课上，我们调整显微镜的镜片来使图像清晰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An understanding of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=optics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>optics</strong></a> helps explain how a microscope lens magnifies a specimen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学知识有助于解释显微镜镜片如何放大标本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=optics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>optics</strong></a> laboratory replaced a damaged lens in its oldest microscope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学实验室更换了最老显微镜中一片损坏的镜片。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">study optics</span> · 研究光学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">magnification</a></span> · 放大率</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=focus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">focus</a></span> · 焦点</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">microscope<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-053" aria-label="定位 microscope">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-053" data-mark-label="microscope" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="microscope：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="microscope：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microscope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 microscope" title="在有道查询 microscope">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-053</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 显微镜</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In optics class, we adjusted the lens of a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microscope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microscope</strong></a> to sharpen the image.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学课上，我们调整显微镜的镜片来使图像清晰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An understanding of optics helps explain how a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microscope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microscope</strong></a> lens magnifies a specimen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学知识有助于解释显微镜镜片如何放大标本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The optics laboratory replaced a damaged lens in its oldest <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microscope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microscope</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学实验室更换了最老显微镜中一片损坏的镜片。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an electron microscope</span> · 电子显微镜</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">magnification</a></span> · 放大率</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=focus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">focus</a></span> · 焦点</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-054">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-054" class="vocabulary-word-title">lens<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-054" aria-label="定位 lens">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-054" data-mark-label="lens" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lens：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lens：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lens&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lens" title="在有道查询 lens">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-054</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 透镜；(照相机等的)镜头</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In optics class, we adjusted the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lens&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lens</strong></a> of a microscope to sharpen the image.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学课上，我们调整显微镜的镜片来使图像清晰。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An understanding of optics helps explain how a microscope <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lens&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lens</strong></a> magnifies a specimen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学知识有助于解释显微镜镜片如何放大标本。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The optics laboratory replaced a damaged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lens&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lens</strong></a> in its oldest microscope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">光学实验室更换了最老显微镜中一片损坏的镜片。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a convex lens</span> · 凸透镜</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">magnification</a></span> · 放大率</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=focus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">focus</a></span> · 焦点</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-055">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-055" class="vocabulary-word-title">radar<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-055" aria-label="定位 radar">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-055" data-mark-label="radar" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="radar：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="radar：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=radar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 radar" title="在有道查询 radar">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-055</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 雷达，无线电定位装置</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=radar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>radar</strong></a> sensor records the echo reflected by an approaching aircraft.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷达传感器记录接近的飞机反射回的回波。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A weak echo reached the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=radar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>radar</strong></a> sensor through heavy rain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">微弱的回波穿过大雨到达雷达传感器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=radar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>radar</strong></a> uses a sensitive sensor to distinguish an aircraft's echo from background noise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷达利用灵敏的传感器将飞机回波与背景噪声区分开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">radar detection</span> · 雷达探测</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detection</a></span> · 探测</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反射</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">echo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-056" aria-label="定位 echo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-056" data-mark-label="echo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="echo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="echo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=echo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 echo" title="在有道查询 echo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 回声；v. 发回声；模仿；重复</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The radar sensor records the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=echo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>echo</strong></a> reflected by an approaching aircraft.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷达传感器记录接近的飞机反射回的回波。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A weak <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=echo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>echo</strong></a> reached the radar sensor through heavy rain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">微弱的回波穿过大雨到达雷达传感器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The radar uses a sensitive sensor to distinguish an aircraft's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=echo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>echo</strong></a> from background noise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷达利用灵敏的传感器将飞机回波与背景噪声区分开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a faint echo</span> · 微弱回波</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detection</a></span> · 探测</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反射</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">sensor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-057" aria-label="定位 sensor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-057" data-mark-label="sensor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sensor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sensor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sensor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sensor" title="在有道查询 sensor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 传感器</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The radar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sensor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sensor</strong></a> records the echo reflected by an approaching aircraft.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷达传感器记录接近的飞机反射回的回波。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A weak echo reached the radar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sensor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sensor</strong></a> through heavy rain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">微弱的回波穿过大雨到达雷达传感器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The radar uses a sensitive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sensor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sensor</strong></a> to distinguish an aircraft's echo from background noise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雷达利用灵敏的传感器将飞机回波与背景噪声区分开。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a motion sensor</span> · 运动传感器</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detection</a></span> · 探测</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反射</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 06.06｜数字设备与信息传递 {#group-06-06}

**串联方式：** 从网络、浏览器与软件进入输入输出设备，再辨认数字、无线、高清和音频；fantasy 是想象延伸。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">multimedia<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-058" aria-label="定位 multimedia">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-058" data-mark-label="multimedia" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="multimedia：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="multimedia：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=multimedia&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 multimedia" title="在有道查询 multimedia">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 多媒体</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The laptop needs updated software and a reliable network to play <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=multimedia&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>multimedia</strong></a> content in its browser.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台笔记本电脑需要更新的软件和可靠的网络，才能在浏览器中播放多媒体内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students use a browser on each laptop to access <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=multimedia&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>multimedia</strong></a> software through the school network.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生在每台笔记本电脑上用浏览器通过校园网络访问多媒体软件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A network fault prevented the laptop browser from loading the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=multimedia&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>multimedia</strong></a> software.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">网络故障使笔记本电脑浏览器无法加载多媒体软件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">multimedia content</span> · 多媒体内容</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=application&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">application</a></span> · 应用程序</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connectivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connectivity</a></span> · 连接能力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-059">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-059" class="vocabulary-word-title">network<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-059" aria-label="定位 network">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-059" data-mark-label="network" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="network：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="network：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=network&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 network" title="在有道查询 network">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-059</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 网络；广播网，电视网</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The laptop needs updated software and a reliable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=network&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>network</strong></a> to play multimedia content in its browser.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台笔记本电脑需要更新的软件和可靠的网络，才能在浏览器中播放多媒体内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students use a browser on each laptop to access multimedia software through the school <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=network&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>network</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生在每台笔记本电脑上用浏览器通过校园网络访问多媒体软件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=network&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>network</strong></a> fault prevented the laptop browser from loading the multimedia software.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">网络故障使笔记本电脑浏览器无法加载多媒体软件。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The laptop needs updated software and a reliable network to play multimedia content in its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=browser&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>browser</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台笔记本电脑需要更新的软件和可靠的网络，才能在浏览器中播放多媒体内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students use a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=browser&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>browser</strong></a> on each laptop to access multimedia software through the school network.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生在每台笔记本电脑上用浏览器通过校园网络访问多媒体软件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A network fault prevented the laptop <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=browser&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>browser</strong></a> from loading the multimedia software.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">网络故障使笔记本电脑浏览器无法加载多媒体软件。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">dial<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-061" aria-label="定位 dial">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-061" data-mark-label="dial" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dial：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dial：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dial" title="在有道查询 dial">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 拨(电话号码)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the fantasy game, players <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dial</strong></a> a number to enter a hidden world.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这款奇幻游戏中，玩家拨打一个号码便能进入隐藏世界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fantasy film shows a child who can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dial</strong></a> the future on an old telephone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这部奇幻电影讲述一个孩子能用旧电话拨号接通未来的故事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Readers enter a fantasy in which they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dial</strong></a> strangers living on another planet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">读者进入一个幻想故事，在其中他们拨号联系生活在另一颗星球的陌生人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dial a number</span> · 拨打号码</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=imagination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">imagination</a></span> · 想象力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=telephone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">telephone</a></span> · 电话</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-062">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-062" class="vocabulary-word-title">fantasy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-062" aria-label="定位 fantasy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-062" data-mark-label="fantasy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fantasy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fantasy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fantasy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fantasy" title="在有道查询 fantasy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-062</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fantasy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fantasy</strong></a> game, players dial a number to enter a hidden world.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这款奇幻游戏中，玩家拨打一个号码便能进入隐藏世界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fantasy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fantasy</strong></a> film shows a child who can dial the future on an old telephone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这部奇幻电影讲述一个孩子能用旧电话拨号接通未来的故事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Readers enter a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fantasy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fantasy</strong></a> in which they dial strangers living on another planet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">读者进入一个幻想故事，在其中他们拨号联系生活在另一颗星球的陌生人。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=laptop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>laptop</strong></a> needs updated software and a reliable network to play multimedia content in its browser.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台笔记本电脑需要更新的软件和可靠的网络，才能在浏览器中播放多媒体内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students use a browser on each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=laptop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>laptop</strong></a> to access multimedia software through the school network.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生在每台笔记本电脑上用浏览器通过校园网络访问多媒体软件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A network fault prevented the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=laptop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>laptop</strong></a> browser from loading the multimedia software.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">网络故障使笔记本电脑浏览器无法加载多媒体软件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a lightweight laptop</span> · 轻便笔记本电脑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=application&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">application</a></span> · 应用程序</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connectivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connectivity</a></span> · 连接能力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">software<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-064" aria-label="定位 software">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-064" data-mark-label="software" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="software：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="software：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=software&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 software" title="在有道查询 software">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 软件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The laptop needs updated <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=software&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>software</strong></a> and a reliable network to play multimedia content in its browser.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这台笔记本电脑需要更新的软件和可靠的网络，才能在浏览器中播放多媒体内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students use a browser on each laptop to access multimedia <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=software&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>software</strong></a> through the school network.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生在每台笔记本电脑上用浏览器通过校园网络访问多媒体软件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A network fault prevented the laptop browser from loading the multimedia <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=software&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>software</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">网络故障使笔记本电脑浏览器无法加载多媒体软件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">install software</span> · 安装软件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=application&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">application</a></span> · 应用程序</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connectivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">connectivity</a></span> · 连接能力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-065">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-065" class="vocabulary-word-title">keyboard<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-065" aria-label="定位 keyboard">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-065" data-mark-label="keyboard" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="keyboard：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="keyboard：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=keyboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 keyboard" title="在有道查询 keyboard">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-065</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 键盘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Connect the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=keyboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>keyboard</strong></a>, screen, microphone and loudspeaker before starting the video call.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">开始视频通话前，连接键盘、屏幕、麦克风和扬声器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The screen displayed instructions while a microphone recorded speech and a loudspeaker played sounds beside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=keyboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>keyboard</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屏幕显示说明，麦克风录制语音，扬声器在键盘旁播放声音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She muted the microphone, lowered the loudspeaker volume and used the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=keyboard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>keyboard</strong></a> to enlarge text on the screen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她关闭麦克风、调低扬声器音量，并用键盘放大屏幕文字。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an ergonomic keyboard</span> · 人体工学键盘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=input&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">input</a></span> · 输入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=output&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">output</a></span> · 输出</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">screen<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-066" aria-label="定位 screen">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-066" data-mark-label="screen" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="screen：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="screen：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=screen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 screen" title="在有道查询 screen">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 屏；屏幕；v. 遮蔽</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Connect the keyboard, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=screen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>screen</strong></a>, microphone and loudspeaker before starting the video call.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">开始视频通话前，连接键盘、屏幕、麦克风和扬声器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=screen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>screen</strong></a> displayed instructions while a microphone recorded speech and a loudspeaker played sounds beside the keyboard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屏幕显示说明，麦克风录制语音，扬声器在键盘旁播放声音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She muted the microphone, lowered the loudspeaker volume and used the keyboard to enlarge text on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=screen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>screen</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她关闭麦克风、调低扬声器音量，并用键盘放大屏幕文字。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a touch screen</span> · 触摸屏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=input&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">input</a></span> · 输入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=output&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">output</a></span> · 输出</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">loudspeaker<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-067" aria-label="定位 loudspeaker">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-067" data-mark-label="loudspeaker" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="loudspeaker：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="loudspeaker：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loudspeaker&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 loudspeaker" title="在有道查询 loudspeaker">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 扬声器，喇叭</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Connect the keyboard, screen, microphone and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loudspeaker&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loudspeaker</strong></a> before starting the video call.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">开始视频通话前，连接键盘、屏幕、麦克风和扬声器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The screen displayed instructions while a microphone recorded speech and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loudspeaker&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loudspeaker</strong></a> played sounds beside the keyboard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屏幕显示说明，麦克风录制语音，扬声器在键盘旁播放声音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She muted the microphone, lowered the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loudspeaker&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loudspeaker</strong></a> volume and used the keyboard to enlarge text on the screen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她关闭麦克风、调低扬声器音量，并用键盘放大屏幕文字。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a portable loudspeaker</span> · 便携扬声器</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=input&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">input</a></span> · 输入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=output&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">output</a></span> · 输出</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">microphone<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-068" aria-label="定位 microphone">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-068" data-mark-label="microphone" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="microphone：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="microphone：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microphone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 microphone" title="在有道查询 microphone">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 话筒，麦克风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Connect the keyboard, screen, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microphone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microphone</strong></a> and loudspeaker before starting the video call.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">开始视频通话前，连接键盘、屏幕、麦克风和扬声器。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The screen displayed instructions while a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microphone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microphone</strong></a> recorded speech and a loudspeaker played sounds beside the keyboard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屏幕显示说明，麦克风录制语音，扬声器在键盘旁播放声音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She muted the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microphone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>microphone</strong></a>, lowered the loudspeaker volume and used the keyboard to enlarge text on the screen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她关闭麦克风、调低扬声器音量，并用键盘放大屏幕文字。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wireless microphone</span> · 无线麦克风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=input&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">input</a></span> · 输入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=output&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">output</a></span> · 输出</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">cassette<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-069" aria-label="定位 cassette">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-069" data-mark-label="cassette" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cassette：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cassette：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cassette&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cassette" title="在有道查询 cassette">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 盒式磁带；底片盒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Digital conversion turns sound on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cassette&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cassette</strong></a> tape into binary data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">数字转换将盒式磁带上的声音变成二进制数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We transferred the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cassette&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cassette</strong></a> tape to a digital file stored as binary information.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们把盒式磁带转换成以二进制信息存储的数字文件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cassette&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cassette</strong></a> tape, a digital recording represents sound using binary numbers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与盒式磁带不同，数字录音用二进制数字表示声音。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cassette player</span> · 盒式磁带播放机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=analogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">analogue</a></span> · 模拟的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digitisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">digitisation</a></span> · 数字化</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">tape<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-070" aria-label="定位 tape">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-070" data-mark-label="tape" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tape：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tape：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tape" title="在有道查询 tape">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 磁带，录音带；胶带</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Digital conversion turns sound on cassette <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tape</strong></a> into binary data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">数字转换将盒式磁带上的声音变成二进制数据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We transferred the cassette <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tape</strong></a> to a digital file stored as binary information.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们把盒式磁带转换成以二进制信息存储的数字文件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike cassette <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tape&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tape</strong></a>, a digital recording represents sound using binary numbers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与盒式磁带不同，数字录音用二进制数字表示声音。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">magnetic tape</span> · 磁带</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=analogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">analogue</a></span> · 模拟的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digitisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">digitisation</a></span> · 数字化</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-073">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-073" class="vocabulary-word-title">wireless<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-073" aria-label="定位 wireless">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-073" data-mark-label="wireless" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wireless：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wireless：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wireless&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wireless" title="在有道查询 wireless">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-073</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 不用电线的，无线的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wireless&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wireless</strong></a> connection supports high-definition video and clear audio.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">稳定的无线连接支持高清视频和清晰音频。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wireless&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wireless</strong></a> receiver sends high-definition pictures and audio to the television.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无线接收器将高清图像和音频传送到电视。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Poor <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wireless&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wireless</strong></a> coverage can interrupt both high-definition streaming and audio playback.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无线信号覆盖不佳可能同时中断高清流媒体和音频播放。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wireless connection</span> · 无线连接</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=streaming&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">streaming</a></span> · 流媒体传输</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bandwidth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bandwidth</a></span> · 带宽</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-074">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-074" class="vocabulary-word-title">high-definition<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-074" aria-label="定位 high-definition">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-074" data-mark-label="high-definition" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="high-definition：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="high-definition：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=high-definition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 high-definition" title="在有道查询 high-definition">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-074</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 高分辨率的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stable wireless connection supports <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=high-definition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>high-definition</strong></a> video and clear audio.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">稳定的无线连接支持高清视频和清晰音频。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wireless receiver sends <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=high-definition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>high-definition</strong></a> pictures and audio to the television.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无线接收器将高清图像和音频传送到电视。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Poor wireless coverage can interrupt both <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=high-definition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>high-definition</strong></a> streaming and audio playback.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无线信号覆盖不佳可能同时中断高清流媒体和音频播放。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">high-definition video</span> · 高清视频</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=streaming&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">streaming</a></span> · 流媒体传输</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bandwidth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bandwidth</a></span> · 带宽</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 06.07｜机械与基础设施怎样运转 {#group-06-07}

**串联方式：** 用泵、发电机、齿轮、枢轴和液压驱动系统，再连接排水、污水和通风。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-076">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-076" class="vocabulary-word-title">pump<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-076" aria-label="定位 pump">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-076" data-mark-label="pump" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pump：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pump：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pump" title="在有道查询 pump">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-076</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 泵；v.(用泵)抽</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The generator powers a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pump</strong></a> that supplies hydraulic pressure to move the gear around its pivot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发电机驱动水泵，提供液压使齿轮绕枢轴运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damaged gear stopped the generator, leaving the hydraulic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pump</strong></a> and central pivot without power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">损坏的齿轮使发电机停转，导致液压泵和中心枢轴失去动力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspect the generator, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pump</strong></a>, gear and pivot before testing the hydraulic system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试液压系统前，检查发电机、泵、齿轮和枢轴。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a water pump</span> · 水泵</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 旋转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanism</a></span> · 机械装置</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-077">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-077" class="vocabulary-word-title">generator<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-077" aria-label="定位 generator">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-077" data-mark-label="generator" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="generator：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="generator：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=generator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 generator" title="在有道查询 generator">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-077</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 发电机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=generator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>generator</strong></a> powers a pump that supplies hydraulic pressure to move the gear around its pivot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发电机驱动水泵，提供液压使齿轮绕枢轴运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damaged gear stopped the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=generator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>generator</strong></a>, leaving the hydraulic pump and central pivot without power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">损坏的齿轮使发电机停转，导致液压泵和中心枢轴失去动力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspect the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=generator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>generator</strong></a>, pump, gear and pivot before testing the hydraulic system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试液压系统前，检查发电机、泵、齿轮和枢轴。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a diesel generator</span> · 柴油发电机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 旋转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanism</a></span> · 机械装置</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">gear<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-078" aria-label="定位 gear">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-078" data-mark-label="gear" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gear：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gear：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gear" title="在有道查询 gear">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 齿轮，传动装置；v. 使适合</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The generator powers a pump that supplies hydraulic pressure to move the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gear</strong></a> around its pivot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发电机驱动水泵，提供液压使齿轮绕枢轴运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damaged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gear</strong></a> stopped the generator, leaving the hydraulic pump and central pivot without power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">损坏的齿轮使发电机停转，导致液压泵和中心枢轴失去动力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspect the generator, pump, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gear</strong></a> and pivot before testing the hydraulic system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试液压系统前，检查发电机、泵、齿轮和枢轴。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a low gear</span> · 低挡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 旋转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanism</a></span> · 机械装置</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">pivot<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-079" aria-label="定位 pivot">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-079" data-mark-label="pivot" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pivot：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pivot：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pivot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pivot" title="在有道查询 pivot">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 枢轴，支点；中心，重点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The generator powers a pump that supplies hydraulic pressure to move the gear around its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pivot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pivot</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发电机驱动水泵，提供液压使齿轮绕枢轴运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damaged gear stopped the generator, leaving the hydraulic pump and central <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pivot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pivot</strong></a> without power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">损坏的齿轮使发电机停转，导致液压泵和中心枢轴失去动力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspect the generator, pump, gear and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pivot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pivot</strong></a> before testing the hydraulic system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试液压系统前，检查发电机、泵、齿轮和枢轴。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a central pivot</span> · 中心枢轴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 旋转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanism</a></span> · 机械装置</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">hydraulic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-080" aria-label="定位 hydraulic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-080" data-mark-label="hydraulic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hydraulic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hydraulic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydraulic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hydraulic" title="在有道查询 hydraulic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-080</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 水力的，液压的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The generator powers a pump that supplies <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydraulic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hydraulic</strong></a> pressure to move the gear around its pivot.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发电机驱动水泵，提供液压使齿轮绕枢轴运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damaged gear stopped the generator, leaving the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydraulic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hydraulic</strong></a> pump and central pivot without power.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">损坏的齿轮使发电机停转，导致液压泵和中心枢轴失去动力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspect the generator, pump, gear and pivot before testing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hydraulic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hydraulic</strong></a> system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">测试液压系统前，检查发电机、泵、齿轮和枢轴。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hydraulic pressure</span> · 液压</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rotation</a></span> · 旋转</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanism</a></span> · 机械装置</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-081">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-081" class="vocabulary-word-title">drainage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-081" aria-label="定位 drainage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-081" data-mark-label="drainage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="drainage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="drainage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drainage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 drainage" title="在有道查询 drainage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-081</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 排水系统；排水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sewage plant requires reliable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drainage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drainage</strong></a> and adequate ventilation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">污水处理厂需要可靠的排水设施及充分通风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors checked <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drainage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drainage</strong></a>, ventilation and sewage disposal in the new building.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查员检查了新建筑的排水、通风和污水处理情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Flooding damaged the sewage pipes, blocked <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drainage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drainage</strong></a> and disabled basement ventilation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪水损坏污水管道、堵塞排水设施，并使地下室通风失效。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a drainage system</span> · 排水系统</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sanitation</a></span> · 卫生设施</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=airflow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">airflow</a></span> · 气流</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-082">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-082" class="vocabulary-word-title">sewage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-082" aria-label="定位 sewage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-082" data-mark-label="sewage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sewage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sewage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sewage" title="在有道查询 sewage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-082</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(下水道的)污水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sewage</strong></a> plant requires reliable drainage and adequate ventilation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">污水处理厂需要可靠的排水设施及充分通风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors checked drainage, ventilation and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sewage</strong></a> disposal in the new building.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查员检查了新建筑的排水、通风和污水处理情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Flooding damaged the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sewage</strong></a> pipes, blocked drainage and disabled basement ventilation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪水损坏污水管道、堵塞排水设施，并使地下室通风失效。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sewage treatment</span> · 污水处理</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sanitation</a></span> · 卫生设施</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=airflow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">airflow</a></span> · 气流</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-083">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-083" class="vocabulary-word-title">ventilation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-083" aria-label="定位 ventilation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-083" data-mark-label="ventilation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ventilation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ventilation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ventilation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ventilation" title="在有道查询 ventilation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-083</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 通风；通风设备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sewage plant requires reliable drainage and adequate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ventilation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ventilation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">污水处理厂需要可靠的排水设施及充分通风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors checked drainage, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ventilation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ventilation</strong></a> and sewage disposal in the new building.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查员检查了新建筑的排水、通风和污水处理情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Flooding damaged the sewage pipes, blocked drainage and disabled basement <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ventilation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ventilation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">洪水损坏污水管道、堵塞排水设施，并使地下室通风失效。</p></li>
</ol>
