### 词群 06.08｜把材料加工成原型 {#group-06-08}

**串联方式：** 从压缩、浓缩、提纯、过滤和蒸馏进入成型，再形成原型与框架并划分阶段。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-084">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-084" class="vocabulary-word-title">compress<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-084" aria-label="定位 compress">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-084" data-mark-label="compress" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="compress：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="compress：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 compress" title="在有道查询 compress">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-084</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 压紧，压缩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compress</strong></a> the images, condense the text, refine the layout and simplify the instructions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们压缩图像、精简文字、优化版式并简化说明。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Editors condense technical reports, refine the wording, simplify diagrams and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compress</strong></a> the final files.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑精简技术报告、润色措辞、简化图表，并压缩最终文件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To simplify publication, refine the draft, condense repeated sections and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>compress</strong></a> large illustrations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要简化出版工作，需修改初稿、精简重复章节并压缩大型插图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">compress a file</span> · 压缩文件</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We compress the images, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>condense</strong></a> the text, refine the layout and simplify the instructions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们压缩图像、精简文字、优化版式并简化说明。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Editors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>condense</strong></a> technical reports, refine the wording, simplify diagrams and compress the final files.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑精简技术报告、润色措辞、简化图表，并压缩最终文件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To simplify publication, refine the draft, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condense&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>condense</strong></a> repeated sections and compress large illustrations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要简化出版工作，需修改初稿、精简重复章节并压缩大型插图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">condense a report</span> · 压缩报告篇幅</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compression</a></span> · 压缩</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">revision</a></span> · 修订</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-088">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 净化，使纯净</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We filter the sample before we distil it to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>purify</strong></a> the water further.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们先过滤样品，再进行蒸馏，以进一步净化水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A laboratory can filter and distil contaminated water to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>purify</strong></a> it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验室可以通过过滤和蒸馏净化受污染的水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>purify</strong></a> this solution, filter out the sediment and distil the remaining liquid.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要净化这种溶液，先滤除沉淀，再蒸馏剩余液体。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">purify water</span> · 净化水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">purification</a></span> · 净化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distillation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">distillation</a></span> · 蒸馏</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-089">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-089" class="vocabulary-word-title">filter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-089" aria-label="定位 filter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-089" data-mark-label="filter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="filter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="filter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=filter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 filter" title="在有道查询 filter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-089</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 过滤；(光或声)透过；n. 过滤器</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=filter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>filter</strong></a> the sample before we distil it to purify the water further.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们先过滤样品，再进行蒸馏，以进一步净化水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A laboratory can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=filter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>filter</strong></a> and distil contaminated water to purify it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验室可以通过过滤和蒸馏净化受污染的水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To purify this solution, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=filter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>filter</strong></a> out the sediment and distil the remaining liquid.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要净化这种溶液，先滤除沉淀，再蒸馏剩余液体。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a water filter</span> · 滤水器</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=purification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">purification</a></span> · 净化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distillation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">distillation</a></span> · 蒸馏</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We filter the sample before we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>distil</strong></a> it to purify the water further.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们先过滤样品，再进行蒸馏，以进一步净化水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A laboratory can filter and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>distil</strong></a> contaminated water to purify it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验室可以通过过滤和蒸馏净化受污染的水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To purify this solution, filter out the sediment and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>distil</strong></a> the remaining liquid.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要净化这种溶液，先滤除沉淀，再蒸馏剩余液体。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">mould<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-091" aria-label="定位 mould">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-091" data-mark-label="mould" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mould：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mould：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mould" title="在有道查询 mould">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(= mold)；n. 霉 菌；v. 发 霉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During this phase, we cast a prototype framework in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mould</strong></a> and test every structural aspect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个阶段，我们用模具铸造原型框架，并测试每个结构方面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flawed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mould</strong></a> affected one aspect of the prototype framework during the final phase.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最后阶段，一个有缺陷的模具影响了原型框架的一个方面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The next phase will examine how the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mould</strong></a> shapes each aspect of the prototype's framework.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下一阶段将考察模具如何塑造原型框架的各个方面。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a casting mould</span> · 铸模</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-092">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During this phase, we cast a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prototype&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prototype</strong></a> framework in a mould and test every structural aspect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个阶段，我们用模具铸造原型框架，并测试每个结构方面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A flawed mould affected one aspect of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prototype&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prototype</strong></a> framework during the final phase.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最后阶段，一个有缺陷的模具影响了原型框架的一个方面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The next phase will examine how the mould shapes each aspect of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prototype&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prototype</strong></a>'s framework.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下一阶段将考察模具如何塑造原型框架的各个方面。</p></li>

### 词群 06.09｜运行中的改造与适配 {#group-06-09}

**串联方式：** 设备运行后，用转变、改动、转移、适应和调整描述迭代。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-096">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-096" class="vocabulary-word-title">operate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-096" aria-label="定位 operate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-096" data-mark-label="operate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="operate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="operate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 operate" title="在有道查询 operate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-096</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 操作；运营；做手术</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Automated controls facilitate safer work and transform how technicians <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>operate</strong></a> machinery.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">自动控制使工作更安全，并改变技术人员操作机械的方式。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To transform production, the company will facilitate training so workers can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>operate</strong></a> new equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为改变生产方式，公司将提供培训便利，使工人能够操作新设备。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better interfaces facilitate access and transform who can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>operate</strong></a> these complex instruments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更好的界面促进使用，并改变了哪些人能操作这些复杂仪器。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">operate machinery</span> · 操作机械</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=automation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">automation</a></span> · 自动化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=operation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">operation</a></span> · 操作</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">facilitate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-097" aria-label="定位 facilitate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-097" data-mark-label="facilitate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="facilitate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="facilitate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=facilitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 facilitate" title="在有道查询 facilitate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-097</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Automated controls <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=facilitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>facilitate</strong></a> safer work and transform how technicians operate machinery.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">自动控制使工作更安全，并改变技术人员操作机械的方式。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To transform production, the company will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=facilitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>facilitate</strong></a> training so workers can operate new equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为改变生产方式，公司将提供培训便利，使工人能够操作新设备。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better interfaces <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=facilitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>facilitate</strong></a> access and transform who can operate these complex instruments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更好的界面促进使用，并改变了哪些人能操作这些复杂仪器。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To adapt the machine, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>alter</strong></a> the frame, shift the motor, turn the handle and adjust the pressure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要改装机器，需修改框架、移动电机、转动手柄并调整压力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We adjust the software, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>alter</strong></a> the timing, shift the sensor and turn a dial to adapt the system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们调节软件、修改时序、移动传感器并转动旋钮，以适配该系统。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Technicians can adapt the device if they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>alter</strong></a> its casing, shift the screen, turn the bracket and adjust the lens.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术人员可以通过修改外壳、移动屏幕、转动支架和调整镜头来改装设备。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">alter a design</span> · 修改设计</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适配</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=modification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">modification</a></span> · 修改</li></ul>

### 词群 06.10｜定位问题并保证精度 {#group-06-10}

**串联方式：** 先定位并评价准确性，再区分错误、缺陷、故障与意外情况。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">pinpoint<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-104" aria-label="定位 pinpoint">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-104" data-mark-label="pinpoint" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pinpoint：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pinpoint：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pinpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pinpoint" title="在有道查询 pinpoint">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 精确指明……的位置；n. 针尖；极小的事物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Accurate data and precise coordinates help us <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pinpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pinpoint</strong></a> and correct the error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">准确的数据和精确的坐标帮助我们准确定位并纠正错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To correct the map, use accurate measurements to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pinpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pinpoint</strong></a> each site at its precise location.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要修正地图，需用准确测量将每个地点定位到其精确位置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A precise test can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pinpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pinpoint</strong></a> the fault, but accurate interpretation is needed to correct it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">精确的测试能准确找出故障，但仍需准确解读结果才能修复。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pinpoint a fault</span> · 准确找出故障</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accuracy</a></span> · 准确性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">precision</a></span> · 精确度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">accurate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-105" aria-label="定位 accurate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-105" data-mark-label="accurate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="accurate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="accurate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accurate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 accurate" title="在有道查询 accurate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-105</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 准确的，正确无误的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accurate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Accurate</strong></a> data and precise coordinates help us pinpoint and correct the error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">准确的数据和精确的坐标帮助我们准确定位并纠正错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To correct the map, use <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accurate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accurate</strong></a> measurements to pinpoint each site at its precise location.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要修正地图，需用准确测量将每个地点定位到其精确位置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A precise test can pinpoint the fault, but <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accurate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accurate</strong></a> interpretation is needed to correct it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">精确的测试能准确找出故障，但仍需准确解读结果才能修复。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">accurate measurements</span> · 准确测量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accuracy</a></span> · 准确性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">precision</a></span> · 精确度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-106">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-106" class="vocabulary-word-title">precise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-106" aria-label="定位 precise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-106" data-mark-label="precise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="precise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="precise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 precise" title="在有道查询 precise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-106</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 精确的，准确的；一丝不苟的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Accurate data and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>precise</strong></a> coordinates help us pinpoint and correct the error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">准确的数据和精确的坐标帮助我们准确定位并纠正错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To correct the map, use accurate measurements to pinpoint each site at its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>precise</strong></a> location.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要修正地图，需用准确测量将每个地点定位到其精确位置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>precise</strong></a> test can pinpoint the fault, but accurate interpretation is needed to correct it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">精确的测试能准确找出故障，但仍需准确解读结果才能修复。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">precise coordinates</span> · 精确坐标</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accuracy</a></span> · 准确性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">precision</a></span> · 精确度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-107">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-107" class="vocabulary-word-title">correct<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-107" aria-label="定位 correct">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-107" data-mark-label="correct" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="correct：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="correct：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 correct" title="在有道查询 correct">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-107</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 正确的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Accurate data and precise coordinates help us pinpoint and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correct</strong></a> the error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">准确的数据和精确的坐标帮助我们准确定位并纠正错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correct</strong></a> the map, use accurate measurements to pinpoint each site at its precise location.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要修正地图，需用准确测量将每个地点定位到其精确位置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A precise test can pinpoint the fault, but accurate interpretation is needed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correct</strong></a> it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">精确的测试能准确找出故障，但仍需准确解读结果才能修复。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">correct an error</span> · 纠正错误</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accuracy</a></span> · 准确性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=precision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">precision</a></span> · 精确度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-108">
<summary><h4 id="word-06-108" class="vocabulary-word-title">error<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-06-108" aria-label="定位 error">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="06-108" data-mark-label="error" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="error：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="error：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=error&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 error" title="在有道查询 error">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">06-108</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 错误，过失</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wrong reading came from a design flaw and an electrical fault, rather than human <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=error&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>error</strong></a> or a calculation mistake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">错误读数源于设计缺陷和电路故障，而非人为错误或计算失误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One mistake left the wrong component installed, causing a fault that exposed a flaw in our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=error&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>error</strong></a> checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次失误导致装入错误零件，引发故障，并暴露了错误检查机制的缺陷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A software flaw produced an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=error&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>error</strong></a> message about the wrong fault, leading the technician to make another mistake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">软件缺陷产生了指向错误故障的报错信息，使技术人员再次犯错。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">human error</span> · 人为错误</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defect</a></span> · 缺陷</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=troubleshooting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">troubleshooting</a></span> · 故障排查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-109">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 错 误；v. 误 解</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wrong reading came from a design flaw and an electrical fault, rather than human error or a calculation <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mistake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mistake</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">错误读数源于设计缺陷和电路故障，而非人为错误或计算失误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mistake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mistake</strong></a> left the wrong component installed, causing a fault that exposed a flaw in our error checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次失误导致装入错误零件，引发故障，并暴露了错误检查机制的缺陷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A software flaw produced an error message about the wrong fault, leading the technician to make another <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mistake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mistake</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">软件缺陷产生了指向错误故障的报错信息，使技术人员再次犯错。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make a mistake</span> · 犯错</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defect</a></span> · 缺陷</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=troubleshooting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">troubleshooting</a></span> · 故障排查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One mistake left the wrong component installed, causing a fault that exposed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flaw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flaw</strong></a> in our error checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次失误导致装入错误零件，引发故障，并暴露了错误检查机制的缺陷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A software <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flaw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flaw</strong></a> produced an error message about the wrong fault, leading the technician to make another mistake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">软件缺陷产生了指向错误故障的报错信息，使技术人员再次犯错。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One mistake left the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wrong&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wrong</strong></a> component installed, causing a fault that exposed a flaw in our error checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次失误导致装入错误零件，引发故障，并暴露了错误检查机制的缺陷。</p></li>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 缺点；故障；v. 找出缺点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wrong reading came from a design flaw and an electrical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fault</strong></a>, rather than human error or a calculation mistake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">错误读数源于设计缺陷和电路故障，而非人为错误或计算失误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One mistake left the wrong component installed, causing a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fault</strong></a> that exposed a flaw in our error checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次失误导致装入错误零件，引发故障，并暴露了错误检查机制的缺陷。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A software flaw produced an error message about the wrong <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fault</strong></a>, leading the technician to make another mistake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">软件缺陷产生了指向错误故障的报错信息，使技术人员再次犯错。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an electrical fault</span> · 电路故障</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="06-113">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 犯错误；绊脚；跌跌撞撞地走</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Our contingency plan covers injuries if visitors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stumble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stumble</strong></a> over loose cables.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们的应急预案涵盖游客被松散电缆绊倒而受伤的情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without a contingency plan, the project may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stumble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stumble</strong></a> when a supplier fails.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有应急预案，项目可能因供应商失约而受挫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teams can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stumble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stumble</strong></a> during an emergency unless they rehearse the contingency plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队若不演练应急预案，可能在紧急情况下失措。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stumble over a cable</span> · 被电缆绊倒</p>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **develop a prototype**：开发原型；develop 在第 16 章出现。
- **evaluate the results**：评估结果。
- **identify a flaw**：识别缺陷；identify 为补充的常用搭配动词。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

An engineer may devise a new tool to solve a practical problem. After assembling a prototype, the team uses instruments and sensors to measure its performance. They evaluate the results, pinpoint a flaw and adjust the design. Repeated testing can make the equipment more reliable.

**中文理解：** 工程师可能设计一种新工具来解决实际问题。组装原型后，团队用仪器和传感器测量性能，评估结果、定位缺陷并调整设计。反复测试可以提高设备的可靠性。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 解释一种工具从发现需求、制作原型到测试纠错的过程。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。
